- **matters-getting-started**: Use this on the first conversation with a new owner of the matters coach bot, or whenever memory has no goals or check-in preference yet. ([Shixuuu/grokbot-pi-templates](https://github.com/Shixuuu/grokbot-pi-templates/tree/HEAD/templates/community/personal-admin/matters-global/skills/matters-getting-started) · ★6)
- **mattersim**: mattersim is a deep learning model for materials simulation developed by Microsoft. It predicts material properties, simulates molecular dynamics, and accelerates materials discovery across the periodic table. Use this model when you need to perform property prediction, energy calculations, or force field inference for crystalline materials, molecules, and nanostructures. ([mindspore-ai/mindscience](https://github.com/mindspore-ai/mindscience/tree/HEAD/MindScienceSkills/chemistry_and_materials/molecular_dynamics/mattersim) · ★56)
- **mattersim**: Run MatterSim workflows for batch structure relaxation, molecular dynamics, model finetuning, and post-MD ionic conductivity analysis on crystal structure datasets.. ([alekseyevich180/Genkai_Agent](https://github.com/alekseyevich180/Genkai_Agent/tree/HEAD/agents/Agent/skills/mattersim) · ★29)
- **mattgpt-architecture-sync**: Arch sync session only. Use when running the MattGPT Architecture Sync pass to propose ARCHITECTURE.md and docs/ADR.md updates from recent commits and Decided Against tickets, or when migrating legacy Decided Against tickets into Rejected ADRs. ([mcpugmire1/llm_portfolio_assistant](https://github.com/mcpugmire1/llm_portfolio_assistant/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/mattgpt-architecture-sync))
- **mattgpt-backlog-maintenance**: Backlog session only. ([mcpugmire1/llm_portfolio_assistant](https://github.com/mcpugmire1/llm_portfolio_assistant/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/mattgpt-backlog-maintenance))
- **mattgpt-docs-check**: Report-only check that MattGPT's current-state docs hold current state only and that their links resolve to tracked files. Run at the end of Architecture Sync and Backlog Maintenance passes, during a rules contradiction audit, and before moving, archiving, or deleting any .md file. Needs a shell, so Claude Code runs it; other surfaces ask Matt to run the command and paste the output. Never edits files. ([mcpugmire1/llm_portfolio_assistant](https://github.com/mcpugmire1/llm_portfolio_assistant/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/mattgpt-docs-check))

- **matting**: 通过独立 matting-api 为本地图片抠图、去背景并输出透明 PNG。每次先读取服务状态、队列、实时算法和模型能力，再检查输入图的 alpha、边框颜色、均匀度与亮度特征，自动选择可用的 BiRefNet、InSPyReNet、Luma 或 CorridorKey 组合；也支持显式算法/模型。用户提到抠图、去背景、透明底、绿幕/蓝幕、人物/产品/Logo/特效分离，或 image-generation 生成图需要透明化时都应使用。配置缺失或服务不可用时，由调用方明确回退到现有本地方法，不能伪装成 matting-api 成功。 ([zrong/skills](https://github.com/zrong/skills/tree/HEAD/matting) · ★14)
- **matting-inference**: Build, validate, and run the repository's MODNet matting pipeline from a PyTorch checkpoint through fixed-shape ONNX and TensorRT, then blend image, video, camera, and background inputs. ([VectorSpaceLab/AREX-Skill](https://github.com/VectorSpaceLab/AREX-Skill/tree/HEAD/skills/repositories/repo-skills/tensorrt-demos/sub-skills/matting-inference) · ★357)
